function UpdateGamepasses()
    -- upvalues: (copy) v_u_1, (ref) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_2, (copy) v_u_126
    local function v186(p185) -- name: v71
        -- upvalues: (ref) v_u_1
        if v_u_1.Gamepasses.Owns(p185:GetAttribute("Gamepass")) then
            if p185:FindFirstChild("Price").Robux:FindFirstChild("Diamonds Gradient") then
                p185:FindFirstChild("Price").Robux:FindFirstChild("Diamonds Gradient"):Destroy()
            end
            if p185:FindFirstChild("Price").Robux:FindFirstChild("UIStroke") then
                p185:FindFirstChild("Price").Robux:FindFirstChild("UIStroke"):Destroy()
            end
            p185:FindFirstChild("Price").Robux.Text = "Owns"
            p185:FindFirstChild("Price").RobuxIcon.Image = "rbxassetid://6516386809"
        else
            UpdateDiamondsFrame(p185:FindFirstChild("Price"), p185:GetAttribute("Gamepass"))
        end
    end
    local function v190(p_u_187) -- name: v75
        -- upvalues: (ref) v_u_1, (ref) v_u_3

                v_u_3 = true

                    local v188, v189 = v_u_1.Network.Invoke("Purchase Gamepass", p_u_187:GetAttribute("Gamepass"))
                    if v188 then
                        v_u_3 = false
                        v_u_1.Audio.Play("rbxassetid://6958723296", script, 1, 1)
                        v_u_1.Message.New("Purchase successful! Thank you so much! :)")
                        return
                    end
                    v_u_1.Message.New(v189 or "Something went wrong.")
                end
                v_u_3 = false
            end
        end)
    end
    for _, v191 in ipairs(v_u_2.Gamepasses:GetChildren()) do
        for _, v192 in ipairs(v191:GetChildren()) do
            if not v192:GetAttribute("Gamepass") then
                continue
            end
            if not v_u_126[v192] then
                v_u_126[v192] = true
                v190(v192)
            end
            v186(v192)
        end
    end

    -- upvalues: (copy) v_u_1, (ref) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_264
    if p301 and not p301:GetAttribute("Setup") then
        p301:SetAttribute("Setup", true)
        v_u_1.GUIFX.ButtonFX(p301)
        p301.Activated:Connect(function()
            -- upvalues: (ref) v_u_3, (ref) v_u_1, (copy) p_u_302, (copy) p_u_303, (ref) v_u_264
            if not v_u_3 then
                v_u_3 = true
                local v304 = v_u_1.Save.Get()
                if v304 and #v304.Pets + p_u_302 <= v304.MaxSlots then
                    local v305 = v_u_264[p_u_303]
                    local v306
                    if v305 then
                        local v307 = v_u_1.FFlags.GetNumber
                        local v308 = tonumber(v307(v305)) or 0
                        v306 = math.floor(v308)
                        if v306 <= 0 or not v306 then
                            local v309 = v_u_1.FFlags.GetNumber
                            local v310 = tonumber(v309("ShopExclusiveEggPrice")) or 0
                            local v311 = math.floor(v310)
                            v306 = v311 > 0 and v311 and v311 or 6000000
                        end
                    else
                        local v312 = v_u_1.FFlags.GetNumber
                        local v313 = tonumber(v312("ShopExclusiveEggPrice")) or 0
                        local v314 = math.floor(v313)
                        v306 = v314 > 0 and v314 and v314 or 6000000
                    end
                    local v315 = v306 * p_u_302
                    if v_u_1.Message.New("Are you sure you want to buy this for " .. v_u_1.Functions.Commas(v315) .. " Diamonds?", true) then
                        local v316, v317 = v_u_1.Network.Invoke("Exclusive Shop: Buy Exclusive Egg", p_u_302, p_u_303)
                        if v316 then
                            v_u_1.Audio.Play("rbxassetid://6958723296", script, 1, 1)
                            v_u_1.Message.New("Purchase successful! Thank you so much! :)")
                        else
                            v_u_1.Message.New(v317 or "Something went wrong. Please try again!")
                        end
                    end
                else
                    v_u_1.Message.New("Your inventory is full!")
                end
                v_u_3 = false
            end
        end)
    end
end
function UpdateSockGift() -- name: UpdateSockGift
    -- upvalues: (copy) v_u_1, (copy) v_u_2, (copy) v_u_289, (ref) v_u_3
    local v_u_319 = v_u_1.Directory.Products["Sock Gifts"].ID
    local v_u_320 = v_u_2.Container.SockGifts.Deal
    v_u_289(v_u_320, "Sock Gift")
    local v_u_321 = v_u_1.Save.Get()
    v_u_1.GUIFX.ButtonFX(v_u_320.Buttons.Price)
    v_u_1.GUIFX.ButtonFX(v_u_320.Buttons.PriceRobux)
    v_u_320.Buttons.Price.Activated:Connect(function()
        -- upvalues: (ref) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_321, (ref) v_u_1
        if not v_u_3 then
            v_u_3 = true
            if #v_u_321.Pets + 3 >= v_u_321.MaxSlots then
                v_u_1.Message.New("You have max pets slots! Delete one first.")
            elseif v_u_1.Message.New("Would you like to buy 3x Gift for 45m Diamonds?", true) then
                local v322, v323 = v_u_1.Network.Invoke("Exclusive Shop: Purchase Gifts", 3)
                if v322 then
                    v_u_3 = false
                    v_u_1.Audio.Play("rbxassetid://6958723296", script, 1, 1)
                    v_u_1.Message.New("Purchase successful! Thank you so much! :)")
                    return
                end
                v_u_1.Message.New(v323 or "Something went wrong.")
            end
            v_u_3 = false
        end
    end)
    v_u_320.Buttons.PriceRobux.Activated:Connect(function()
        -- upvalues: (ref) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_321, (ref) v_u_1, (copy) v_u_319
        if not v_u_3 then
            v_u_3 = true
            if #v_u_321.Pets + 3 >= v_u_321.MaxSlots then
                v_u_1.Message.New("You have max pets slots! Delete one first.")
            else
                v_u_1.Functions.PromptPurchase(v_u_319, true)
            end
            v_u_3 = false
        end
    end)
    coroutine.wrap(function()
        -- upvalues: (copy) v_u_320, (ref) v_u_1
        local v324 = v_u_320.Egg
        local v325 = v324.Position
        while true do
            v_u_1.Functions.Tween(v324, {
                ["Position"] = v325 - UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.05, 0)
            }, { 0.4, "Sine", "InOut" }).Completed:Wait()
            v_u_1.Functions.Tween(v324, {
                ["Position"] = v325
            }, { 0.4, "Sine", "InOut" }).Completed:Wait()
        end
    end)()
end

        while true do

            }, { 0.4, "Sine", "InOut" }).Completed:Wait()
        end
    end)()

local function v_u_440() -- name: TickExclusiveOrnateEgg
    -- upvalues: (copy) v_u_2, (copy) v_u_1, (copy) v_u_394
    local v422 = v_u_2.Container:FindFirstChild("ExclusiveEggPS99")
    if v422 then
        local v423 = v422:FindFirstChild("Deal2")
        local v424
        if v423 then
            v424 = v423:FindFirstChild("Subtitle")
        else
            v424 = v423
        end
        local v425 = v_u_1.FFlags.GetNumber
        local v426 = tonumber(v425("ShopExclusiveHarvestF2PEndTime")) or 0
        local v427 = math.floor(v426)
        local v428 = v_u_1.FFlags.GetNumber
        local v429 = tonumber(v428("ShopExclusiveHarvestF2PEndTime")) or 0
        local v430 = math.floor(v429)
        local v431
        if v430 > 0 then
            v431 = v430 <= workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
        else
            v431 = false
        end
        if v431 then
            if v423 and v423.Visible or v422:FindFirstChild("Pages") and v422.Pages.Visible then
                v_u_394(v422, 1)
            end
        elseif v424 then
            v424.Visible = v427 > 0
            if v427 > 0 then
                local v432 = v427 - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                local v433 = math.floor(v432)
                local v434 = math.max(0, v433)
                local v435 = v434 / 3600
                local v436 = math.floor(v435)
                local v437 = v434 % 3600 / 60
                local v438 = math.floor(v437)
                local v439 = v434 % 60
                v424.Text = "Limited Time for " .. string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", v436, v438, v439) .. "!"
            end
        end
    else
        return
    end
end

function UpdateHugeBalloonCat(p517)

        v519.Visible = false

function Scrollbar() -- name: Scrollbar
    -- upvalues: (copy) v_u_2
    local v542 = v_u_2.Container:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
    local v543 = v_u_2.Container:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
    local v544 = v542.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + v543.PaddingTop.Offset + v543.PaddingBottom.Offset
    v_u_2.Container.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, v544)
end
function SizeFrame(p545) -- name: SizeFrame
    -- upvalues: (copy) v_u_1
    local v546 = v_u_1.Functions.ResolutionScale()
    local v547 = 1
    for _, v548 in ipairs(p545:GetChildren()) do
        if v548.ClassName == "UIPadding" then
            local v549 = v_u_1.GUIFX.CacheProperty(v548, "PaddingTop")
            local v550 = v_u_1.GUIFX.CacheProperty(v548, "PaddingBottom")
            v548.PaddingTop = UDim.new(v549.Scale, v549.Offset * v546)
            v548.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(v550.Scale, v550.Offset * v546)
            v547 = v547 + v548.PaddingTop.Offset + v548.PaddingBottom.Offset
        else
            if v548.ClassName ~= "UIListLayout" and v548.ClassName ~= "UIGridLayout" then
                continue
            end
            v547 = v547 + v548.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
            if v548.ClassName == "UIListLayout" then
                local v551 = v_u_1.GUIFX.CacheProperty(v548, "Padding")
                v548.Padding = UDim.new(v551.Scale, v551.Offset * v546)
            elseif v548.ClassName == "UIGridLayout" then
                local v552 = v_u_1.GUIFX.CacheProperty(v548, "CellPadding")
                v548.CellPadding = UDim2.new(v552.X.Scale, v552.X.Offset * v546, v552.Y.Scale, v552.Y.Offset * v546)
            end
            if v548.ClassName ~= "UIGridLayout" or not v548:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIAspectRatioConstraint") then
                continue
            end
            v548.CellSize = UDim2.new(v548.CellSize.X.Scale, v548.CellSize.X.Offset, 0, v548.AbsoluteCellSize.Y + 2)
        end
    end
    p545.Size = UDim2.new(p545.Size.X.Scale, p545.Size.X.Offset, 0, v547)
end
function Scaling()
    -- upvalues: (copy) v_u_2
    if v_u_2.Gui.Enabled then
        local v553 = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.Y <= 1000
        v_u_2.Frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIAspectRatioConstraint").AspectRatio = v553 and 1.6 or 1.2
        v_u_2.Tabs.Size = v553 and UDim2.new(0.365, 0, 0.1, 0) or UDim2.new(0.365, 0, 0.065, 0)
        for _, v554 in ipairs(v_u_2.Container:GetChildren()) do
            if not v554:IsA("GuiObject") then
                continue
            end
            for _, v555 in ipairs(v554:GetChildren()) do
                if v555:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout") then
                    SizeFrame(v555)
                else
                    if not v555:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGridLayout") then
                        continue
                    end
                    SizeFrame(v555)
                end
            end
            SizeFrame(v554)
        end
        Scrollbar()
    end
end
coroutine.wrap(function()
    -- upvalues: (copy) v_u_1
    while true do
        v_u_1.Heartbeat()
        Scaling()
    end
end)()

    end
end

            local v570 = v568:FindFirstChild(v569)

                v570 = v570:FindFirstChild("Pets")
            end
            for _, v571 in ipairs(v_u_566) do
                local v572
                if v570 then
                    v572 = v570:FindFirstChild(v571.Slot)
                else

                end
                if v572 then

                if v572 then

            end
        end
    end

            UpdateOctupleEgg()

            UpdateMVPDeal()
        elseif p574 == "AreasUnlocked" or p574 == "Debug" then

        end
    end
end)
